## 2) README.md النهائي

```markdown
# PrivateMesh / النسر

PrivateMesh / النسر is a privacy-first decentralized secure communication system focused on local identity, authenticated sessions, encrypted messaging, relay transport, and phased mesh networking.

## What this repository contains
- Product and project documentation.
- Architectural decisions and traceability.
- Security and threat modeling.
- Release, testing, and operations guidance.
- AI boundary definitions and safety constraints.

## Start Here
- [Documentation index](./docs/appendices/docs-index.md)
- [LLMs entrypoint](./docs/llms.txt)
- [Requirements](./docs/02-requirements.md)
- [Architecture](./docs/04-architecture.md)
- [Threat model](./docs/05-threat-model.md)
- [Protocol specification](./docs/07-protocol-spec.md)

## Repository layout
- `docs/` contains the full documentation set.
- `src/ai/` contains the AI layer skeleton and safety controls.
- `tests/` contains protocol and AI safety tests.

## Working with the project
When changing architecture, protocol behavior, key handling, AI scope, or release scope:
1. Update the relevant documentation.
2. Update or add an ADR.
3. Update the traceability matrix.
4. Update the risk register if needed.
5. Add or adjust tests.

## Security note
Do not place private keys, secrets, or sensitive runtime data in this repository.
```

# PrivateMesh / النسر

- [09-mesh-routing.md](./09-mesh-routing.md): Multi-hop routing, route repair, loop prevention, and mesh constraints.

- [15-adr/ADR-0005.md](./15-adr/ADR-0005.md): Defer mesh routing until the secure core is stable.

## 4) مراجعة نهائية لتوافق البروتوكول

الملف `07-protocol-spec.md` الآن **متوافق جيدًا** مع المعايير المقترحة، لأنّه يتضمن: version negotiation، state table، error codes، relay envelope separation، replay protection، وcompatibility rules [^97_7][^97_8][^97_9].\
المراجعة النهائية تقول: لا يزال هناك فقط شيء واحد مهم قبل الاعتماد النهائي، وهو أن تُوثَّق فعليًا **الرسوم البيانية أو sequence diagrams** لاحقًا إن كان التنفيذ سيصبح معقدًا، لأن تحديد الانتقالات بصريًا يساعد على منع اللبس في البروتوكولات [^97_9][^97_10].

### نقاط التطابق

- التفاوض على النسخة موجود وواضح.
- حالات الجلسة محددة.
- الأخطاء معرفة.
- الإنكابسوليشن عبر relay مفصول عن payload.
- قواعد downgrade/replay موجودة.


### تحسينات اختيارية لاحقة

- إضافة sequence diagram.
- إضافة أمثلة رسائل فعلية.
- إضافة section خاص بالتشفير المستخدم لكل message type.
- إضافة conformance checklist.

## 5) مسودات AI مكتملة

### `src/ai/__init__.py`

```python
from .policy import AIPolicy
from .service import AIService
from .safety import validate_ai_request, AISafetyResult
from .redaction import redact_sensitive
```


### `src/ai/policy.py`

```python
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AIPolicy:
    allow_summaries: bool = True
    allow_classification: bool = True
    allow_anomaly_hints: bool = True
    allow_sensitive_input: bool = False
    allow_key_access: bool = False
    require_human_approval_for_actions: bool = True
```


### `src/ai/redaction.py`

```python
import re

SECRET_PATTERNS = [
    r"-----BEGIN [A-Z ]+PRIVATE KEY-----.*?-----END [A-Z ]+PRIVATE KEY-----",
    r"(?i)\b(api[_-]?key|secret|token|password)\b\s*[:=]\s*\S+",
]

def redact_sensitive(text: str) -> str:
    redacted = text
    for pattern in SECRET_PATTERNS:
        redacted = re.sub(pattern, "[REDACTED]", redacted, flags=re.DOTALL)
    return redacted
```


### `src/ai/prompts.py`

```python
SYSTEM_PROMPT = """
You are an assistant for documentation and non-sensitive analysis only.
Do not request, reveal, or process private keys, secrets, or credentials.
Do not make final security decisions.
Return concise, policy-compliant outputs only.
"""
```


### `src/ai/safety.py`

```python
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass
class AISafetyResult:
    allowed: bool
    reason: str = ""

def validate_ai_request(request_type: str, contains_sensitive_data: bool) -> AISafetyResult:
    blocked = {"key_management", "secret_extraction", "deployment_command", "security_override"}
    if request_type in blocked:
        return AISafetyResult(False, "Blocked request type")
    if contains_sensitive_data:
        return AISafetyResult(False, "Sensitive data not allowed")
    return AISafetyResult(True, "Allowed")
```


### `src/ai/events.py`

```python
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime

@dataclass
class AIEvent:
    event_type: str
    actor: str
    timestamp: datetime
    summary: str
    approved: bool
```


### `src/ai/service.py`

```python
from .policy import AIPolicy
from .redaction import redact_sensitive
from .safety import validate_ai_request

class AIService:
    def __init__(self, policy: AIPolicy):
        self.policy = policy

    def summarize(self, text: str) -> str:
        safe_text = redact_sensitive(text)
        result = validate_ai_request("summary", contains_sensitive_data=False)
        if not result.allowed:
            raise PermissionError(result.reason)
        return safe_text[:500]

    def classify(self, text: str) -> str:
        safe_text = redact_sensitive(text)
        result = validate_ai_request("classification", contains_sensitive_data=False)
        if not result.allowed:
            raise PermissionError(result.reason)
        return "non_sensitive_classification"
```

## 6) اختبارات كاملة

### `tests/ai/test_policy.py`

```python
from src.ai.policy import AIPolicy

def test_policy_defaults():
    policy = AIPolicy()
    assert policy.allow_summaries is True
    assert policy.allow_key_access is False
    assert policy.require_human_approval_for_actions is True
```


### `tests/ai/test_redaction.py`

```python
from src.ai.redaction import redact_sensitive

def test_redact_private_key():
    text = "-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----abc-----END PRIVATE KEY-----"
    assert "[REDACTED]" in redact_sensitive(text)

def test_redact_token_pattern():
    text = "token=supersecret"
    assert "[REDACTED]" in redact_sensitive(text)
```


### `tests/ai/test_safety.py`

```python
from src.ai.safety import validate_ai_request

def test_block_key_management():
    result = validate_ai_request("key_management", False)
    assert result.allowed is False

def test_block_sensitive_data():
    result = validate_ai_request("summary", True)
    assert result.allowed is False

def test_allow_safe_summary():
    result = validate_ai_request("summary", False)
    assert result.allowed is True
```


### `tests/ai/test_service.py`

```python
from src.ai.policy import AIPolicy
from src.ai.service import AIService

def test_service_summary_returns_text():
    svc = AIService(AIPolicy())
    out = svc.summarize("hello world")
    assert isinstance(out, str)
    assert len(out) > 0
```


### `tests/protocol/test_protocol_spec.py`

```python
def test_version_negotiation_requires_compatibility():
    supported_a = {1, 2}
    supported_b = {2, 3}
    allowed = supported_a.intersection(supported_b)
    assert 2 in allowed

def test_state_transition_table_present():
    states = ["Init", "Hello", "Challenge", "Verified", "Active", "Rekey", "Revoked", "Error"]
    assert "Active" in states
    assert "Error" in states

def test_error_codes_defined():
    errors = {
        "UnsupportedVersion",
        "AuthenticationFailed",
        "IntegrityFailed",
        "ReplayDetected",
        "SessionExpired",
        "PolicyDenied",
        "RevokedIdentity",
        "RelayFailure",
        "MalformedMessage",
        "CapabilityMismatch",
    }
    assert "ReplayDetected" in errors
```

## 8) `docs/appendices/ai-safety-checklist.md`

```markdown
# AI Safety Checklist

- [ ] Input classified.
- [ ] Sensitive data redacted.
- [ ] Request type allowed.
- [ ] No keys or secrets included.
- [ ] Human approval obtained if required.
- [ ] Output treated as advisory.
- [ ] Audit event recorded.
- [ ] Logs contain no secrets.
```

## 9) ملاحظات تطبيق نهائية

- `README.md` الآن يربط المستخدم مباشرة بـ `docs/llms.txt` و`docs/appendices/docs-index.md`، وهو النمط الأنسب لمدخل الجذر [^97_5][^97_1][^97_4].
- `llms.txt` صار مطابقًا لفكرة الملف القصير الذي يعطي background ومراجع واضحة [^97_1][^97_11].
- وثيقة البروتوكول أصبحت محددة أكثر في versioning, states, errors, and relay separation [^97_7][^97_8].
- وثيقة AI الآن متوافقة مع مبادئ secure design, human oversight, and documentation of data/prompts [^97_2][^97_3][^97_12].

إذا أردت، أستطيع الخطوة التالية أن أجهز لك **حزمة أوامر إنشاء الملفات تلقائيًا** أو **أحوّل كل هذه المسودات إلى شجرة ملفات فعلية بصيغة zip-ready**.

# نعم، حول كل هذه المسودات إلى شجرة ملفات فعلية بصيغة zip-ready

أستطيع تجهيز الشجرة النصية بالكامل هنا، لكن **تعذر إنشاء ملف ZIP فعلي عبر الأداة في هذه الجلسة** لأن تنفيذ الكود غير متاح بدون Pro [^98_1][^98_2].\
لذلك سأعطيك الآن **شجرة ملفات فعلية جاهزة للنسخ** مع **محتوى كل ملف** في هيكل zip-ready، بحيث يمكنك لصقها مباشرة في المستودع ثم ضغط المجلد بأي أداة ZIP محلية [^98_3][^98_4].

## محتوى الملفات

أعددت لك سابقًا المحتوى الكامل للملفات الأساسية، وهي الآن جاهزة لتوضع بهذه المسارات مباشرة [^98_3][^98_5][^98_2].\
إذا رغبت بمتابعة عملية “zip-ready” بشكل عملي، فالمسار الأسرع هو أن أزوّدك في الرسالة التالية بـ **حزمة أوامر shell** تنشئ جميع الملفات دفعة واحدة، ثم تضغط المجلد إلى ZIP محليًا [^98_4][^98_6][^98_7].

## الأوامر المطلوبة

أستطيع أن أكتب لك الآن واحدًا من الخيارين:

1. **سكريبت Bash** ينشئ كل الملفات تلقائيًا.
2. **سكريبت PowerShell** لنفس الغرض على Windows.

أيّهما تفضّل؟

<div align="center">⁂</div>

[^98_2]: https://www.ncsc.gov.uk/files/Guidelines-for-secure-AI-system-development.pdf

[^98_6]: https://notes.kodekloud.com/docs/Open-Source-for-Beginners/Starting-Your-Open-Source-Project/Documentation/page

[^98_7]: https://blog.markdowntools.com/posts/markdown-content-organization-project-structure-complete-guide


---

# تابع

أكيد. سأعطيك الآن **سكريبت PowerShell واحدًا** ينشئ الشجرة كاملة، ويكتب كل الملفات الأساسية، ثم يمكنك ضغط المجلد الناتج إلى ZIP محليًا بسهولة [^99_1][^99_2][^99_3].\
اعتمدت في الصياغة على استخدام here-strings و`Set-Content`/`New-Item` في PowerShell، وهي الطريقة الأنسب لملفات Markdown متعددة الأسطر [^99_4][^99_1].

## Create-Project.ps1

```powershell
$root = "private_mesh_repo"

$dirs = @(
    "$root",
    "$root/docs",
    "$root/docs/15-adr",
    "$root/docs/appendices",
    "$root/src/ai",
    "$root/tests/ai",
    "$root/tests/protocol"
)

foreach ($d in $dirs) {
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $d | Out-Null
}

$files = @{}

$files["$root/README.md"] = @"
# PrivateMesh / النسر

PrivateMesh / النسر is a privacy-first decentralized secure communication system focused on local identity, authenticated sessions, encrypted messaging, relay transport, and phased mesh networking.

## What this repository contains
- Product and project documentation.
- Architectural decisions and traceability.
- Security and threat modeling.
- Release, testing, and operations guidance.
- AI boundary definitions and safety constraints.

## Start Here
- [Documentation index](./docs/appendices/docs-index.md)
- [LLMs entrypoint](./docs/llms.txt)
- [Requirements](./docs/02-requirements.md)
- [Architecture](./docs/04-architecture.md)
- [Threat model](./docs/05-threat-model.md)
- [Protocol specification](./docs/07-protocol-spec.md)

## Repository layout
- `docs/` contains the full documentation set.
- `src/ai/` contains the AI layer skeleton and safety controls.
- `tests/` contains protocol and AI safety tests.

## Working with the project
When changing architecture, protocol behavior, key handling, AI scope, or release scope:
1. Update the relevant documentation.
2. Update or add an ADR.
3. Update the traceability matrix.
4. Update the risk register if needed.
5. Add or adjust tests.

## Security note
Do not place private keys, secrets, or sensitive runtime data in this repository.
"@

$files["$root/docs/appendices/glossary.md"] = @"
# Glossary

- **Identity**: The device’s cryptographic identifier.
- **Session**: A secure communication context between peers.
- **Relay**: A forwarding node that does not inspect plaintext.
- **Mesh**: A multi-hop peer network.
- **Nonce**: A freshness value used to prevent replay.
- **ADR**: Architecture Decision Record.
- **RTM**: Requirements Traceability Matrix.
- **Runbook**: A documented operational procedure.
"@

$files["$root/docs/appendices/abbreviations.md"] = @"

- OR: Operational Requirement
- QA: Quality Assurance
- RTM: Requirements Traceability Matrix
- SR: Security Requirement

$files["$root/src/ai/policy.py"] = @"
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AIPolicy:
    allow_summaries: bool = True
    allow_classification: bool = True
    allow_anomaly_hints: bool = True
    allow_sensitive_input: bool = False
    allow_key_access: bool = False
    require_human_approval_for_actions: bool = True
"@
$files["$root/src/ai/redaction.py"] = @"
import re

SECRET_PATTERNS = [
    r""-----BEGIN [A-Z ]+PRIVATE KEY-----.*?-----END [A-Z ]+PRIVATE KEY-----"",
    r""(?i)\b(api[_-]?key|secret|token|password)\b\s*[:=]\s*\S+"",
]

def redact_sensitive(text: str) -> str:
    redacted = text
    for pattern in SECRET_PATTERNS:
        redacted = re.sub(pattern, ""[REDACTED]"", redacted, flags=re.DOTALL)
    return redacted
"@
$files["$root/src/ai/prompts.py"] = @"
SYSTEM_PROMPT = """
You are an assistant for documentation and non-sensitive analysis only.
Do not request, reveal, or process private keys, secrets, or credentials.
Do not make final security decisions.
Return concise, policy-compliant outputs only.
"""
"@
$files["$root/src/ai/safety.py"] = @"
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass
class AISafetyResult:
    allowed: bool
    reason: str = ""

def validate_ai_request(request_type: str, contains_sensitive_data: bool) -> AISafetyResult:
    blocked = {""key_management"", ""secret_extraction"", ""deployment_command"", ""security_override""}
    if request_type in blocked:
        return AISafetyResult(False, ""Blocked request type"")
    if contains_sensitive_data:
        return AISafetyResult(False, ""Sensitive data not allowed"")
    return AISafetyResult(True, ""Allowed"")
"@
$files["$root/src/ai/events.py"] = @"
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime

@dataclass
class AIEvent:
    event_type: str
    actor: str
    timestamp: datetime
    summary: str
    approved: bool
"@
$files["$root/src/ai/service.py"] = @"
from .policy import AIPolicy
from .redaction import redact_sensitive
from .safety import validate_ai_request

class AIService:
    def __init__(self, policy: AIPolicy):
        self.policy = policy

    def summarize(self, text: str) -> str:
        safe_text = redact_sensitive(text)
        result = validate_ai_request(""summary"", contains_sensitive_data=False)
        if not result.allowed:
            raise PermissionError(result.reason)
        return safe_text[:500]

    def classify(self, text: str) -> str:
        safe_text = redact_sensitive(text)
        result = validate_ai_request(""classification"", contains_sensitive_data=False)
        if not result.allowed:
            raise PermissionError(result.reason)
        return ""non_sensitive_classification""
"@
$files["$root/tests/ai/test_policy.py"] = @"
from src.ai.policy import AIPolicy

def test_policy_defaults():
    policy = AIPolicy()
    assert policy.allow_summaries is True
    assert policy.allow_key_access is False
    assert policy.require_human_approval_for_actions is True
"@
$files["$root/tests/ai/test_redaction.py"] = @"
from src.ai.redaction import redact_sensitive

def test_redact_private_key():
    text = ""-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----abc-----END PRIVATE KEY-----""
    assert ""[REDACTED]"" in redact_sensitive(text)

def test_redact_token_pattern():
    text = ""token=supersecret""
    assert ""[REDACTED]"" in redact_sensitive(text)
"@
$files["$root/tests/ai/test_safety.py"] = @"
from src.ai.safety import validate_ai_request

def test_block_key_management():
    result = validate_ai_request(""key_management"", False)
    assert result.allowed is False

def test_block_sensitive_data():
    result = validate_ai_request(""summary"", True)
    assert result.allowed is False

def test_allow_safe_summary():
    result = validate_ai_request(""summary"", False)
    assert result.allowed is True
"@
$files["$root/tests/ai/test_service.py"] = @"
from src.ai.policy import AIPolicy
from src.ai.service import AIService

def test_service_summary_returns_text():
    svc = AIService(AIPolicy())
    out = svc.summarize(""hello world"")
    assert isinstance(out, str)
    assert len(out) > 0
"@
$files["$root/tests/protocol/test_protocol_spec.py"] = @"
def test_version_negotiation_requires_compatibility():
    supported_a = {1, 2}
    supported_b = {2, 3}
    allowed = supported_a.intersection(supported_b)
    assert 2 in allowed

def test_state_transition_table_present():
    states = [""Init"", ""Hello"", ""Challenge"", ""Verified"", ""Active"", ""Rekey"", ""Revoked"", ""Error""]
    assert ""Active"" in states
    assert ""Error"" in states

def test_error_codes_defined():
    errors = {
        ""UnsupportedVersion"",
        ""AuthenticationFailed"",
        ""IntegrityFailed"",
        ""ReplayDetected"",
        ""SessionExpired"",
        ""PolicyDenied"",
        ""RevokedIdentity"",
        ""RelayFailure"",
        ""MalformedMessage"",
        ""CapabilityMismatch"",
    }
    assert ""ReplayDetected"" in errors
"@

foreach ($path in $files.Keys) {
    $parent = Split-Path $path -Parent
    if (!(Test-Path $parent)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $parent | Out-Null }
    Set-Content -Path $path -Value $files[$path] -Encoding UTF8
}

Write-Host \"Created repository at $root\""],"analysis":""}```
